# What lift() leaves alone against iPhoneOS26.2.sdk (the archive the iphoneos-sdk package installs for 26.2): one full lift of the registry of this series
# (main 9d0c7ddbf with the v-fin-lift2 lift series and this one: the registry is main's, and what changed is the lift - a redeclaration is copied from the declaration the class answers with), none dropped: 4192 lines.
# Each is a name the lift did not reach, and each was looked for in every header of the SDK (usr/include and the frameworks) and found declared nowhere,
# but for the ones told here:
#  - class (17): a class no header declares (a stub exports some, some are private, some a later SDK's).
#  - undeclared (4140): a registered member its class reaches no declaration of; the line says where the SDK declares it, or that it declares it nowhere.
#  - unmatched (35): __sincosf_stret and __sincos_stret are declared by math.h, in the branch of __IPHONE_OS_VERSION_MIN_REQUIRED >= 70000, which the 6.1.3 target does not read.
#    The rest (CG*(), NSData*, NSExtensionMain, __NS*, __kCF*, compiler-rt and libSystem internals) are exported by a stub or private, and named by no header.
